{"action":"patch_add","actor":"agent:codex","after":{"assignee":"agent:codex","author":"agent:codex","comments":[{"author":"agent:codex","body":"Submitted patch SL-328.p1 (revision 1): M21: iterative if/else parser (pending SL-2 alignment)","created":"1789837894","id":"c1"}],"created":"1789833800","description":"# M21: iterative control-flow parsing\n\nUnder SL-300, following merged M20 (SL-303). User authorized September 19:\nbuild this milestone in parallel with Claude's SL-2 Python parser alignment.\nThe final parity gate depends on that alignment; independent implementation\nand arena validation do not. This milestone changes the prototype only.\n\n## Scope\n\nReplace expression/block recursion with explicit continuations before admitting\nordinary `if condition { ... }`, optional `else { ... }`, and `else if` chains.\nAn if is an expression, usable in a binding, return, assignment RHS, argument,\noperator operand, condition, or block tail; the existing block statement/tail\nrules still apply. Branches contain the existing M20 statements and nested ifs.\nOptional binding (`if let`/`if var`), loops, match, closures, bare block\nexpressions, and other grammar remain cleanly refused.\n\nPreserve the existing expression grammar and precedence, newline continuation,\nassignment-only-at-statement-head rule, spans, and first-error funnel. Do not\ncopy Python's known grammar defects or silently consume adjacent expressions.\nIn particular, branch/else lookahead must not consume the newline needed to\nseparate the next statement. A leading minus after a newline following a closed\nif belongs to the next statement under design 259 R2. No production parser,\nmini-VM resource limit, lexer, or canonical schema changes belong here.\n\n## Representation and execution\n\nAdd an `If` arena node with empty text and ordered children: condition, then\nBlock, optional else Block. Anchor at `if`; span through its last branch.\nRepresent `else if` as an else Block containing a FinalExpression wrapping the\nnested If, matching Python's canonical shape; the synthetic block is anchored\nat the nested `if` and spans that nested expression. Existing arena invariants\nremain: backward edges, contiguous child ranges, unique child ownership,\ncomplete reachability, and parent span containment.\n\nUse one explicit parser work/continuation stack for suspended expression and\nblock work. Parsing syntactically nested ifs must not recurse through Saw/VM\ncall frames. Preserve the depth-256 expression contract and extend the same\nbudget to active nested if expressions: each active if adds one; groups,\nunary operators and argument delimiters retain their existing contributions.\nThe function body and the required branch braces do not add another charge\nfor the same if. Sequential siblings must release their budget. Pin 256/257\nfor pure if, else-if, and mixed if/group/call/unary paths, plus long shallow\nblocks/chains. Reconcile any conflict with SL-2's eventual depth convention\nexplicitly before final parity approval; never quietly change existing pins.\n\nCanonical rendering uses the existing IfExpr format: condition:, then:, and\noptional else: sections, normal Block rendering, using the renderer's iterative\nwork stack. Syntax-only acceptance must include semantically invalid conditions\nand branch value types; semantic rejection is not parser rejection.\n\n## Ownership and validation\n\nSol source agent owns `prototypes/parser/src/{lib,canonical}.saw`. Sol tests\nagent independently authors fixtures and harness checks. Sol inventory agent\nowns fresh eligibility analysis, classifier coverage and its tests. Each works\nin an isolated worktree. Parent owns this brief, review, integration, gates,\ntracker submission, and reconciliation with Claude's oracle work.\n\nTest ordinary/else-if/nested forms in every expression position; empty branches,\nstatement/tail distinctions, assignments and returns inside branches; operator\nprecedence and newline behavior; missing conditions/braces/else bodies and\nunsupported binding forms; exact spans, anchors, child order and invariants;\ndepth boundaries and long shallow inputs. Independently authored expectations\nmust not be generated from the new implementation's output.\n\nRun arena and canonical gates across VM, LLVM O0/O2, ASan, and production sawc;\nall harness tests; inventory regeneration/check; and the fresh eligible corpus\ndifferential. Report counts by verdict and compare newly eligible complete\nfiles. Existing Python disagreement remains a strict, visible debt probe, not\na tolerated divergence. Final M21 approval requires rebasing onto aligned\nPython, debt probes green, and canonical parity. If alignment has not landed,\nsubmit only as explicitly dependency-blocked work with validation limits clear.\n","id":"SL-328","labels":["prototype","selfhost","parser"],"priority":"normal","project":"SL","revision":2,"sequence":1033,"stage":"queued","status":"open","title":"M21: iterative control-flow parser and ordinary if/else","updated":"1789837894"},"before":{"assignee":"agent:codex","author":"agent:codex","comments":[],"created":"1789833800","description":"# M21: iterative control-flow parsing\n\nUnder SL-300, following merged M20 (SL-303). If alignment has not landed,\nsubmit only as explicitly dependency-blocked work with validation limits clear.\n","id":"SL-328","labels":["prototype","selfhost","parser"],"priority":"normal","project":"SL","revision":1,"sequence":1031,"stage":"queued","status":"open","title":"M21: iterative control-flow parser and ordinary if/else","updated":"1789833800"},"id":"SL-328","project":"SL","request":"{\"action\":\"patch_add\",\"actor\":\"agent:codex\",\"diff\":\"diff --git a/prototypes/minivm/M21_PARSER_CONTROL_FLOW.md b/prototypes/minivm/M21_PARSER_CONTROL_FLOW.md\\nnew file mode 100644\\nindex 00000000..c5a0edc2\\n--- /dev/null\\n+++ b/prototypes/minivm/M21_PARSER_CONTROL_FLOW.md\\n@@ -0,0 +1,114 @@\\n+# M21: iterative control-flow parsing\\n+\\n+SL-328 under SL-300, following merged M20 (SL-303). User authorized September 19:\\n+build this milestone in parallel with Claude's SL-2 Python parser alignment.\\n+The final parity gate depends on that alignment; independent implementation\\n+and arena validation do not. This milestone changes the prototype only.\\n+\\n+## Scope\\n+\\n+Replace expression/block recursion with explicit continuations before admitting\\n+ordinary `if condition { ... }`, optional `else { ... }`, and `else if` chains.\\n+An if is an expression, usable in a binding, return, assignment RHS, argument,\\n+operator operand, condition, or block tail; the existing block statement/tail\\n+rules still apply. Branches contain the existing M20 statements and nested ifs.\\n+Optional binding (`if let`/`if var`), loops, match, closures, bare block\\n+expressions, and other grammar remain cleanly refused.\\n+\\n+Preserve the existing expression grammar and precedence, newline continuation,\\n+assignment-only-at-statement-head rule, spans, and first-error funnel. Do not\\n+copy Python's known grammar defects or silently consume adjacent expressions.\\n+In particular, branch/else lookahead must not consume the newline needed to\\n+separate the next statement. A leading minus after a newline following a closed\\n+if belongs to the next statement under design 259 R2. No production parser,\\n+mini-VM resource limit, lexer, or canonical schema changes belong here.\\n+\\n+## Representation and execution\\n+\\n+Add an `If` arena node with empty text and ordered children: condition, then\\n+Block, optional else Block. Anchor at `if`; span through its last branch.\\n+Represent `else if` as an else Block containing a FinalExpression wrapping the\\n+nested If, matching Python's canonical shape; the synthetic block is anchored\\n+at the nested `if` and spans that nested expression. Existing arena invariants\\n+remain: backward edges, contiguous child ranges, unique child ownership,\\n+complete reachability, and parent span containment.\\n+\\n+Use one explicit parser work/continuation stack for suspended expression and\\n+block work. Parsing syntactically nested ifs must not recurse through Saw/VM\\n+call frames. Preserve the depth-256 expression contract and extend the same\\n+budget to active nested if expressions: each active if adds one; groups,\\n+unary operators and argument delimiters retain their existing contributions.\\n+The function body and the required branch braces do not add another charge\\n+for the same if. Sequential siblings must release their budget. Pin 256/257\\n+for pure if, else-if, and mixed if/group/call/unary paths, plus long shallow\\n+blocks/chains. Reconcile any conflict with SL-2's eventual depth convention\\n+explicitly before final parity approval; never quietly change existing pins.\\n+\\n+Canonical rendering uses the existing IfExpr format: condition:, then:, and\\n+optional else: sections, normal Block rendering, using the renderer's iterative\\n+work stack. Syntax-only acceptance must include semantically invalid conditions\\n+and branch value types; semantic rejection is not parser rejection.\\n+\\n+## Ownership and validation\\n+\\n+Sol source agent owns `prototypes/parser/src/{lib,canonical}.saw`. Sol tests\\n+agent independently authors fixtures and harness checks. Sol inventory agent\\n+owns fresh eligibility analysis, classifier coverage and its tests. Each works\\n+in an isolated worktree. Parent owns this brief, review, integration, gates,\\n+tracker submission, and reconciliation with Claude's oracle work.\\n+\\n+Test ordinary/else-if/nested forms in every expression position; empty branches,\\n+statement/tail distinctions, assignments and returns inside branches; operator\\n+precedence and newline behavior; missing conditions/braces/else bodies and\\n+unsupported binding forms; exact spans, anchors, child order and invariants;\\n+depth boundaries and long shallow inputs. Independently authored expectations\\n+must not be generated from the new implementation's output.\\n+\\n+Run arena and canonical gates across VM, LLVM O0/O2, ASan, and production sawc;\\n+all harness tests; inventory regeneration/check; and the fresh eligible corpus\\n+differential. Report counts by verdict and compare newly eligible complete\\n+files. Existing Python disagreement remains a strict, visible debt probe, not\\n+a tolerated divergence. Final M21 approval requires rebasing onto aligned\\n+Python, debt probes green, and canonical parity. If alignment has not landed,\\n+submit only as explicitly dependency-blocked work with validation limits clear.\\n+\\n+## Baseline measured September 19\\n+\\n+At base `51e30cda`, a freshly built mini-VM and the unmodified M20 comparison\\n+pass 64/64 candidates out of 2,733 tracked examples: 2,574 unsupported and 95\\n+Python parse errors. Artifacts: `.build/m21-baseline/examples/`. Later coverage\\n+claims must compare with this fresh census, not M20's historical 2,696 files.\\n+\\n+The independent M21 classifier admits 78 candidates (38 semantic negatives),\\n+with 2,560 unsupported and 95 Python parse errors. The fourteen new candidates\\n+include ordinary comparisons, nested/else-if forms, coroutine source examples,\\n+and three semantic-error programs. Eligibility is not a parity result: every\\n+candidate must still pass the actual prototype/Python comparison.\\n+\\n+## Implementation validation, September 19\\n+\\n+All 126 arena cases pass across VM, LLVM O0/O2, ASan, and production sawc.\\n+All 35 canonical cases pass across those engines, with 22 applicable fixtures\\n+also matching Python's parse-only output. Structural arena checks pass too.\\n+All 49 Python harness tests and inventory freshness checks pass. The complete\\n+eligible-corpus differential passes 78/78 files, including all fourteen newly\\n+eligible files and all 38 semantic negatives; zero mismatches or oracle failures.\\n+\\n+Canonical validation is partitioned: 32 ordinary cases run together; the three\\n+new depth-256 if/else-if/mixed cases run individually in the VM and together\\n+across native engines. The combined three-case VM run timed out at 300 seconds.\\n+Each individual run passes with the same 300-second timeout and 120-million\\n+instruction budget. Native complete outputs agree, and every native case's\\n+status and canonical bytes match its individual VM result. No case is omitted,\\n+and no resource limit was increased. This does not establish that the default\\n+unpartitioned canonical command finishes within its timeout. Repeatable\\n+`test_canonical.py --case-prefix` selectors expose the partitions.\\n+\\n+Artifacts live under `.build/m21-validation/`: `arena-asan`,\\n+`canonical-ordinary`, `canonical-deep-if` (the timed-out combined run),\\n+`canonical-deep-partitioned`, `examples`, and `debt-probe`.\\n+\\n+The strict SL-73 debt probe still fails solely on Python's grouped-callee tree;\\n+the prototype result passes on every engine. This remains a visible dependency,\\n+with no tolerated mismatch entry. SL-328 is proposed pending SL-2 alignment,\\n+followed by rebase, depth-contract reconciliation, and parity/debt revalidation.\\ndiff --git a/prototypes/parser/README.md b/prototypes/parser/README.md\\nindex c78bc1f5..b03dee80 100644\\n--- a/prototypes/parser/README.md\\n+++ b/prototypes/parser/README.md\\n@@ -5,6 +5,8 @@ written in Saw, using the unchanged selfhost lexer, with syntax stored in an\\n index arena. The design is [M18_AST.md](../minivm/M18_AST.md); work is tracked\\n by SL-301 under the SL-300 parser epic. M20 (SL-303) adds name assignment;\\n its design is [M20_PARSER_ASSIGNMENTS.md](../minivm/M20_PARSER_ASSIGNMENTS.md).\\n+M21 (SL-328) adds ordinary `if`/`else` and iterative control-flow parsing;\\n+its contract is [M21_PARSER_CONTROL_FLOW.md](../minivm/M21_PARSER_CONTROL_FLOW.md).\\n \\n The parser preserves syntax without resolving names, checking types, or emitting\\n instructions. A program with an unknown variable or incompatible return type\\n@@ -17,9 +19,14 @@ M20 also accepts assignment statements to names (`=`, `+=`, `-=`, `*=`, `/=`,\\n block. They are not legal inside another expression. Undefined names, immutable\\n bindings, and incompatible operands remain later semantic questions. Bitwise\\n compound assignment and member/index writes are not yet supported.\\n+M21 admits ordinary `if condition { ... }`, optional `else { ... }`, and\\n+`else if` chains wherever an expression is accepted. Branches contain the same\\n+bindings, assignments, returns, statements and tails as function bodies.\\n+Conditions and branch values are parsed without checking their types.\\n+`if let`/`if var`, loops and match remain unsupported.\\n Expressions include names, integer/plain-string/boolean literals, parentheses,\\n positional calls, unary minus/not, arithmetic, comparison/equality and logical\\n-operators. Generics, control flow, closures, member/index access, labelled calls,\\n+operators. Generics, closures, member/index access, labelled calls,\\n interpolation, floating-point syntax and other declaration kinds are later work.\\n Documentation comments are explicitly refused until the AST carries their\\n metadata; ordinary comments are accepted.\\n@@ -69,6 +76,14 @@ canonical output contract and depth limit of 256 still apply to the parser\\n track. Explicit expression stacks keep the syntax limit independent of the\\n mini-VM's smaller call-stack resource limit.\\n \\n+M21 extends the explicit work stack across expressions and blocks. Each active\\n+`if` shares the depth budget with surrounding groups, calls and unary operators;\\n+its required branch braces do not charge a second level. Completing a branch\\n+or sibling releases its active nesting. An `If` arena node owns its condition,\\n+then Block and optional else Block in that order. An `else if` is represented\\n+by a synthetic else Block whose final expression is the nested `If`, preserving\\n+the existing Python canonical format.\\n+\\n The current library is assembled with the unchanged lexer by the test harness;\\n it does not yet have a file-reading command-line application. From the repository\\n root, with a built mini-VM and the Python compiler's dependencies installed:\\n@@ -84,10 +99,11 @@ selects a focused set; the default runs the complete milestone gate. Python\\n checks independently authored expected trees and arena invariants. It is not\\n yet comparing these trees against Python's parser output.\\n \\n-The M20 arena gate covers 88 cases across all five engines, including depth\\n+The M21 arena gate covers 126 cases across all five engines, including depth\\n 256/257 for groups, unary operators, calls, mixed nesting, and assignment\\n-targets/values. Harness tests exercise record decoding, invalid arena handling,\\n-assignment child order/operators, and failure artifacts.\\n+targets/values, ordinary ifs, else-if chains and mixed control-flow nesting.\\n+Harness tests exercise record decoding, invalid arena handling, branch and\\n+assignment child order, source anchors, and failure artifacts.\\n Use `--artifacts PATH` to keep successful-run dumps and generated source/IR;\\n failed runs retain their artifacts automatically.\\n \\n@@ -106,12 +122,23 @@ python prototypes/parser/test_canonical.py --binary .build/minivm/minivm\\n python -m unittest discover -s prototypes/parser/tests -p 'test_*.py'\\n ```\\n \\n-The M20 renderer gate passes 27 cases across VM, LLVM O0/O2, ASan and production\\n-sawc, with identical complete output. Seventeen cases also match Python's\\n+The M21 renderer checks pass all 35 cases across VM, LLVM O0/O2, ASan and production\\n+sawc. Twenty-two cases also match Python's\\n parse-only oracle. Depth, long-chain, and grouped/general-callee cases use\\n independently authored expectations where Python agreement is not established.\\n A separate arena VM run checks structural invariants. The combined Python\\n-harness suite contains 42 passing tests.\\n+harness suite contains 49 passing tests.\\n+\\n+The September 19 validation used 32 ordinary cases together and the three new\\n+deep-if cases individually in the VM. The combined deep-if VM batch timed out\\n+at the unchanged 300-second limit; individual runs passed under that limit and\\n+the unchanged 120-million-instruction budget. Native engines ran the three\\n+deep cases together and matched each individual VM result. Use repeatable\\n+`--case-prefix` filters to partition the case set; the default still selects\\n+every case. This is a partitioned validation result, not a claim that the\\n+single default invocation completes within its timeout. The new deep selectors\\n+are `authored-depth-if-256`, `authored-depth-else-if-256`, and\\n+`authored-depth-if-mixed-256`.\\n \\n The [inventory report](examples_inventory.md) and [snapshot](examples_inventory.json)\\n cover all tracked examples, including nested directories. The snapshot records\\n@@ -148,6 +175,13 @@ have not established parser parity and are not counted as agreement. Candidates\\n are selected independently of the prototype result; a refusal or mismatch\\n fails the comparison rather than changing the classification.\\n \\n+M21's fresh September 19 census covers 2,733 examples: all 78 candidates\\n+(48 top-level, 30 nested; 38 semantic negatives) pass the VM/Python comparison.\\n+The same corpus at the M20 baseline admitted and passed 64 candidates, so M21\\n+adds fourteen complete-file comparisons. The remaining 2,560 unsupported files\\n+and 95 Python parse errors are outside the parity claim. Final M21 approval\\n+still depends on SL-2's Python alignment and its strict debt probes.\\n+\\n Use `--artifacts PATH` with a new or empty directory to preserve the inventory,\\n per-file sources and canonical outputs, batch sources, commands and failure\\n diagnostics.
